european_nation(N) :- nation(N,_,R), region(R,europe).
european_supplier(S) :- supplier(S,N), european_nation(N).
brass_part(P) :- part(P,_,_,_,_,_,brass,15,_,_).
q2_part(P) :- brass_part(P), partsupp(P,S), european_supplier(S).
q2 :- q2_part(_).
query(q2).
